--- entity_reviews [row 2 of 5] ---
id: d88d0bcc-d2a2-4135-9bcd-97d8a6cf56cc
entity_slug: ruby-slipper-orange-beach
reviewer_name: Amanda Major
rating: 5
body: The Ruby Slipper Cafe on Perdido Beach Blvd in Orange Beach, Alabama, is open from breakfast through lunch. The chain originated in New Orleans, and they say they add some New Orleans flair to all their dishes. Some of their most popular orders include Blackstone and Cochon Eggs, BBQ Shrimp & Grits, and Bananas Foster French Toast. For those wanting something more potent to go with your meal, they have  Bloody Marys or Mimosas. The decor is cute, with space for dining inside and out, and the bathrooms are clean. I seem to dine here on girls trips. Our servers have always been polite and efficient, with knowledge of their large menu. I’m a pancake fan, and I seem to eat a different flavor during every visit. Pay attention to their operating hours and know they take reservations, which can be made online. Our meals have always been great here.
